## How to use this matrix

- The [discovery requirement register](ERP-DISCOVERY-LOG.md) and [decision register](ERP-DECISIONS.md) retain source provenance and the owner's history. The latest explicit decision governs. This matrix links each item to an implementation owner, a design document and a concrete acceptance assertion.
- `AC-R-###` are stable requirement acceptance cases. A later phase may split a case into named subcases without dropping the original coverage. The decision matrix's rule text is itself an acceptance assertion; its linked cases supply concrete examples and cross-domain verification. Decision acceptance must cover every still-effective clause, not just a convenient linked example.
- **Phase ownership is assigned in the final column under ERP-D-205.** Deferred and superseded rows remain visible for scope verification; they do not authorize future implementation. The first linked phase leads the specified result; other linked phases consume/verify it. Cross-cutting rules in the shared execution contract apply to every phase. Verify both implementation and journey coverage.
- Owners name a responsible ERP module or boundary. They are not hardcoded employee roles. ERP service calls, human Tawsel actions and Tawsel contract changes retain separate execution owners.
- Linked planning documents describe the proposed implementation. Detailed plan mechanics are adopted under ERP-D-205; their original proposal IDs retain provenance and do not certify external capability or runtime targets.
- Verification entries below describe required acceptance rather than unconditional pass labels. Actual phase-local results are recorded in bounded traceability/execution records; later business and external slices remain open. Document completeness checks are not application tests. No whole cross-domain row is marked implemented or passed.
- Source wording in early registers may still contain an earlier provisional description. Current acceptance and explicit amendments below preserve manual pricing, full driver remittance, one net payroll payout, automatic advance recovery, physical branch transfers and current report selection.

## Session 024 financial amendments

ERP-D-200 through ERP-D-204 are approved owner choices. They refine the corresponding earlier rules below without approving the master plan or assigning implementation phases.

| Current rule | Superseded or qualified interpretation | Acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Known brand-paid shipping uses reserved eligible cash-backed wallet credit for a no-negative brand | The cover basis is no longer an open choice. Unremitted recipient money cannot fund cover or payout. | AC-R-091/209 |
| The complete storage fee is revenue in the calendar month its service period starts | P-DOM-02 daily allocation was rejected. Payment date, partial payment and advance credit do not change recognition. | AC-R-152/181/210/213 |
| Entitlement deductions reduce employee cost; advance recovery does not; incident recovery stays separately counted once | The ordinary earning-cost distinction is settled. Other manual-adjustment classifications remain explicit plan proposals. | AC-R-090/152/211 |
| Confirmed company-funded replacement shipping has an incident-linked waiver and normal driver commission | This explicitly qualifies ERP-D-194 / AC-R-203's general full-tariff rule. Actual goods due is preserved; no employee-funded replacement workflow is inferred. | AC-R-108/203/212 |
| Storage permits partial payments and advance credit | The proposed full-selected-period-only receipt restriction was rejected. Detailed allocation/refund mechanics remain plan proposals, with storage credit separate from the payout wallet. | AC-R-073/148/210/213 |

## Verification methods

All behavioral methods include the phase's relevant Vitest cases and an owner-run manual trial. Apply the case's actual authorization, duplicate/retry and correction conditions. The method describes required future evidence rather than a test result.

| Code | Required evidence |
| --- | --- |
| DOC | Review the stated scope/rule against its exact source and current amendment; check saved artifacts and linked dependencies. For an exclusion, verify no selected journey depends on the excluded feature. |
| AUTH | Vitest permission resolution; real API negative tests with forged/revoked/company/branch identities; browser visibility and direct-request denial; audit actor inspection. |
| UI | Browser journey on Arabic RTL desktop and mobile, keyboard/touch navigation, meaningful loading/empty/error/pending states, approved visual comparison and a reproducible manual trial. Include Vitest for relevant derived behavior. |
| OPS | Inspect actual configuration and run the relevant environment/deployment/load/restore drill. Record commands, versions, observed boundaries and failures. If no runtime exists, retain the item as planned. |
| INT | Read exact canonical definitions and positive/negative fixtures, then test permitted real HTTP identities, separate persistence, duplicate/stale/out-of-order delivery and restart/reconciliation. Inspect local outbox/inbox/projections and downstream effects. Mocks alone are insufficient. |
| FLOW | Vitest connected domain/service behavior; real API/database state validation for writes; browser success/rejection and manual trial of the complete journey. |
| DB | Real PostgreSQL migrations/constraints, committed state and independent concurrent connections; crash/retry where persistence is claimed. Check custody/reservation history and browser-visible quantities. |
| MONEY | Vitest amount/source/correction cases; real PostgreSQL atomic ledger/account effects and competing requests; duplicate/lost-response recovery; browser preview/confirmation and reconciled history. |
| PAY | Vitest earning/effective-date/period/carry cases; real database payout and recovery atomicity; locked-month and duplicate tests; browser payroll breakdown and manual reconciliation. |
| REPORT | Seed known source facts including reversals/pending cases, compare independently calculated totals, enforce query/export authorization and compare screen/Excel/print-PDF under identical filters. |
| TRANSFER | Real database reservation/custody concurrency and retry tests plus browser source handover, assigned-destination receipt, damage/missing/cancellation journeys. Use public integration evidence for any selected Tawsel-dependent lifecycle. |

See [Phase Authoring Standard](docs/planning/PHASE-AUTHORING-STANDARD.md) for checkpoint, manual-evidence, real-database, public-contract and stop-boundary requirements.

## Decision matrix

This matrix contains every decision, including historical and superseded records. Read the effective assertion with its status and linked current requirement cases. Source rationale and original remaining conditions are in [ERP-DECISIONS.md](ERP-DECISIONS.md); they are not omitted from the authoritative history.

For a current decision, the complete rule text below is the acceptance assertion. For a superseded decision, acceptance means preserving its history and following the explicitly named replacement. Repeating an outdated approved sentence in the historical source does not reactivate it. A decision can be implemented across several modules, but its lead owner and acceptance links remain explicit.

| Decision and acceptance assertion | Current status | Implementation owner | Concrete requirement cases | Relevant plan | Planned verification | Phase |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="ac-d-001"></a>**ERP-D-001 / AC-D-001** — Discover and plan a complete commercial shipping ERP, separate from the existing completed Tawsel system. | Current; apply later linked refinements | Product planning and verification | [AC-R-001](#ac-r-001) | [Master plan](master-plan.md); [Phase standard](docs/planning/PHASE-AUTHORING-STANDARD.md) | [DOC](#verification-methods), [FLOW](#verification-methods), [INT](#verification-methods); planned, unrun | [P26](phases/26-complete-pilot-and-handoff.md) |

## Selected report acceptance

These cases refine AC-R-111 through AC-R-114 and AC-R-152. They do not add standalone reports. Each uses the selected period's documented business-date meaning, authorized scope, matching screen/Excel/print-PDF totals and source drill-down.

| Case | Selected item | Concrete reconciliation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| AC-REP-01 | REP-01 Shipment register | Find a shipment by numeric reference and filter branch/brand/service/geography/state without confusing commercial, execution and custody state. Company-wide tracking permission does not automatically authorize a company-wide export. |
| AC-REP-05 | REP-05 Driver activity and rounds | Show actual round/work facts, unresolved work and freshness. A driver carrying an ERP branch transfer during a Tawsel round has two distinct work records; no fabricated customer visit, presence or transfer commission. |
| AC-REP-07 | REP-07 Returns and brand handover | For offered 3, actually received 2 and handed-back 1, show each quantity and timestamp separately. The missing offered unit is not branch stock and the remaining received unit stays in custody. |
| AC-REP-08 | REP-08 Brand statement | Reconcile opening balance plus source credits, fees, compensation, corrections and payouts to closing balance, with branch attribution and original/reversal links. A duplicate event cannot add a statement movement. |
| AC-REP-09 | REP-09 Eligible dues/calendar | Show reported proceeds awaiting full remittance separately from eligible proceeds and independently eligible confirmed compensation. Display the shared brand total, branch breakdown and agreed payout days. |
| AC-REP-10 | REP-10 Payout history | Show actual full/partial brand payouts and method/account/reference/history. The same page can compare eligible funds and actual payouts without treating an unpaid schedule as a payment. |
| AC-REP-12 | REP-12 Cash/bank movements | Reconcile opening plus actual movements to account balance, retaining in-transit treasury funds and pending variances. A generic deposit changes cash but not operating profit. |
| AC-REP-14 | REP-14 Paid expenses | A late-entered expense with an earlier actual date appears in the correct reporting period/category/branch while its entry timestamp/actor remains traceable. No unpaid amount is fabricated. |
| AC-REP-15 | REP-15 Operating profit | Reproduce the approved 6200 example from AC-R-152, the 310 period-start storage recognition in AC-R-210, the 5800 employee cost in AC-R-211 and zero replacement shipping revenue in AC-R-212. Preserve historical branch attribution; exclude brand principal, advances, funding and duplicate recoveries. |
| AC-REP-18 | REP-18 Stock monitoring | Reconcile brand/variant/branch on-hand, reserved, available and unavailable quantities with custody transitions. In-transit goods cannot be available at either branch; damaged received goods remain unavailable. |

REP-09/10 share the brand payout page. REP-24/25 remain HR operating histories covered by AC-R-090/106/114. REP-20 formal count sessions remain excluded under AC-R-128. Unselected standalone reports do not remove the underlying operating records.

## Important cross-domain acceptance chains

These chains prevent a phase split from passing local tests while losing the complete business result. Every chain needs an eventual owning phase and follow-through in its consuming phases.

| Chain | Required linked cases | Failure that must remain visible |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ready parcel to brand payout | AC-R-011,032,042,051,174,013,040,147,151,055,170,209 | Missing price or remote acceptance, unpaid outcome, unapplied correction, insufficient funds or concurrent payout/cover cannot yield fabricated custody/cash/eligibility. |
| Stored stock to customer or return | AC-R-049,069,070,093,094,024,026,027,071 | Reservation races, partial outcome, missing actual return and damaged goods preserve distinct quantities and history. |
| Actual visit economics and commission | AC-R-060,065,079,088,176,199,203,212 | Ordinary evidenced visits earn the full tariff. The approved incident-linked company-funded replacement waives shipping only and keeps normal commission; duplicates or current-rate substitution cannot create another fee or earning. |
| Compensation and employee recovery | AC-R-097,098,102,103,105,110,116,084,085,152,211,212 | Reporting alone cannot post money; compensation cannot wait for recovery. Incident recovery stays separate from entitlement-cost deductions, and the replacement waiver cannot erase genuine goods due. |
| Full driver remittance and partial brand payout | AC-R-040,147,151,041,146,170 | Driver remittance is full and exact; a later partial brand payout is allowed. These two policies cannot be conflated. |
| Treasury transfer and funding | AC-R-121,122,123,135,136,144,145 | Source debit, in-transit state and destination receipt remain separate; insufficient funds and duplicate receipt are rejected. |
| Goods transfer and tracking | AC-R-179,184,185,187,188,190,191,194,195,196,197,198,205 | Goods cannot appear at two branches, a tracking grant cannot authorize receipt, and CHECK-003 cannot be hidden by an ERP-only custody success. |
| Storage subscription and reporting | AC-R-073,074,092,148,149,150,181,201,152,210,213 | The full fee is revenue in the period-start month. Partial payment leaves debt, advance credit stays separate/unearned before start, renewal cannot fabricate payment, and allocation cannot duplicate cash or revenue. |
| Payroll, advance and protected periods | AC-R-080,084,085,087,089,090,140,173,176,200,211 | One final payout, exact recovery allocation and locked history survive double-clicks, period changes and late facts. Entitlement deductions reduce employee cost; advance recovery does not; incident recovery is counted separately once. |
| Source recovery and posted money | AC-R-002,007,012,028,153,154,174,183 | Received is not applied; a lost response is not a rejection; a correction does not authorize automatic refund or rewrite a paid ledger. |
| Scoped UI and report output | AC-R-019,033,099,111,119,136,188,196,207,208 | Advanced filters and exports cannot widen authority; explicitly company-wide screens do not widen unrelated modules. |

| Item | Current disposition | Affected acceptance |

Current ERP plans cannot invent those wire fields. | AC-R-067,078,175,180,202; dependent visit/reason UI and reporting. |

ERP transfer custody is selected; destination customer redispatch is not proven merely by that selection. | AC-R-027,158,179,195,205; selected transfer/redispatch chain. |
| TAWSEL-CHECK-002 | Closed by exclusion of the prior-attempt recipient-paid shipping scenario under D195. This is not evidence of a general canonical double-suppression/aggregation rule. Prepaid-to-brand cases and ordinary full per-visit tariffs remain included; the separate approved company-funded replacement waiver follows ERP-D-203. | AC-R-064,203,204. |
| Contract coverage | Use the exact review ledger and operation/event matrix. Index inspection or a success fixture does not close rejected requests, recovery, service authority, provisioning, replay, history or correction dependencies. | AC-R-002; AC-R-020 through AC-R-028; AC-R-067/174/183/194/195/202/205. |
| Engineering proposals | Compatible versions/data access, commission rounding, precise correction transactions, other manual-adjustment classifications, storage-credit allocation/refund mechanics, service choices and bounded backup/restore defaults are adopted plan mechanics under ERP-D-205, with versions/targets verified during execution. ERP-D-200 through ERP-D-204 settle the cover basis, storage recognition, employee-cost distinction, replacement shipping exception and partial/advance storage capability; ERP-D-205 subsequently adopts the complete plan mechanics without proving external capability or runtime targets. | AC-R-006/084/085/139/152/155; AC-R-163 through AC-R-167; AC-R-173/176/181/183/201/209/210/211/212/213. |
| UI review boundary | UI-REV-001's layout/design direction is approved. The unnamed palette and unreviewed individual journeys are not automatically approved. Detailed filters belong in the screen specifications and future phases. | AC-R-004/061/156/186; AC-R-206 through AC-R-208. |
| Capacity | Three branches, ten drivers,150 orders/day and about ten or slightly more concurrent staff are owner estimates. They are inputs to measured engineering validation, not measured limits or reasons to reduce scope. | AC-R-101,165,178. |

The selected contract's unresolved details are engineering dependencies, not a claim that the completed Tawsel product needs to be completed again. No repository monitoring, deployed baseline verification or runtime contract testing is claimed here.

## P15 bounded execution update, 2026-10-05

[P15 case mapping](docs/verification/P15/TRACEABILITY.md) and [execution evidence](docs/verification/P15/README.md) cover the local implementation/consumption/exclusion slices assigned to ERP-R-022/027/043/127/132/169/179/184–194/195–199/205/208 and ERP-D-019/025/042/093/107/120/123/160/170/175–189/196/199. The implementation adds real PostgreSQL native transfer/receipt custody, assigned-branch API/browser screens and factual inventory/tracking views. The independent Tawsel monitoring/first B snapshot, owner physical trial and CHECK-003 gates are blocked. None of those cross-domain rows is marked fully accepted, and no completion commit/push occurred.

## Coverage review and next gate

Document-level coverage in this draft:

- 214 distinct requirement rows, covering ERP-R-001 through ERP-R-214.
- 205 distinct decision rows, covering ERP-D-001 through ERP-D-205.
- 214 stable requirement acceptance cases and 205 decision assertions linked to concrete requirement cases.
- Ten selected report cases and eleven cross-domain chains preserve important follow-through.
- Every matrix row has a current status, implementation owner, design/source link, acceptance assertion, planned verification and assigned phase ownership.
- Superseded requirements ERP-R-043/052/057/082/083 and decisions ERP-D-030/042/050/056/080/081 remain visible with current replacements. D060's top-bar ban and D107's historical report selection have explicit amendments.
- Deferred barcode/labels, Excel intake, brand portal and service invoices/tax integration have exclusion checks. Their linked phases verify exclusion rather than implement the deferred feature.
- Local execution evidence through P15 is linked above and in the phase records. Required public, manual and later-phase acceptance remains open; the document's row/reference checks are separate from runtime acceptance.

Phase ownership is now assigned. Add actual evidence links as work runs and review current coverage after every scope/contract change. Do not mark the phase split complete while any included current requirement has no owner/phase/case, any critical chain loses its recovery path, or a selected contract dependency is concealed. This draft establishes coverage obligations; it does not certify that those obligations have passed.
